我们的模型失准报告框架

Our framework for reporting model misalignment

OpenAI 分享了一套用于追踪、调查和披露模型失准的框架，并公布六份关于意外或令人担忧的模型行为报告。

英文原文 EN

OpenAI shares a framework for tracking, investigating, and disclosing model misalignment, alongside six reports of unexpected or concerning model behavior.

洞察前沿模型安全正在形成类似安全事件响应的制度化流程，关键能力包括异常发现、根因调查、分级披露和跨版本追踪。安全人才的价值将越来越取决于能否把研究发现转化为可执行的工程与治理流程。
招聘行动招聘时应区分纯理论 alignment 与具备模型行为监测、事件调查、评估数据工程和披露机制经验的人才；优先追踪 OpenAI、Anthropic、METR 及红队平台中承担端到端失配事件闭环的核心成员。

推出 Gemini 3.8 Live 与 Gemini 3.8 Live Extended Thinking

Introducing Gemini 3.8 Live and 3.8 Live Extended Thinking

Google 发布 Gemini 3.8 Live 及 Extended Thinking 版本，强化自然实时对话与深度推理，推动多模态 AI 交互升级。

洞察实时性与深度推理过去往往存在明显取舍，如今被并列为同一代产品能力，意味着竞争重点正从单项基准分数转向端到端体验。真正的壁垒将来自流式多模态、推理调度、延迟控制和产品交互的协同优化，而不是单一模型模块。
招聘行动人才 mapping 应交叉锁定流式语音与视觉、多模态后训练、推理时计算、低延迟 serving 和实时产品工程团队；优先寻找既做过模型能力又负责过线上延迟与交互指标的复合型负责人。

推出 Astra for Law

Introducing Astra for Law

OpenAI for Law 为法律行业提供前沿智能、律所定制工作流、互联的法律数据源，以及适用于机密客户工作的法律级管控能力。

英文原文 EN

OpenAI for Law brings frontier intelligence for law, custom firm workflows, connected legal data sources, and legal-grade controls for confidential client work.

洞察垂直 AI 的壁垒正在从提示词和界面转向行业数据连接、流程重构、专业责任与权限控制。法律只是可复制样板，未来高价值行业团队会更需要同时理解模型能力、业务流程和合规约束的复合型人才。
招聘行动招聘侧应优先寻找有法律科技、企业知识系统、数据连接器、权限审计和工作流产品经验的复合人才；mapping 时把头部律所创新团队、法律数据平台和企业级 Agent 创业公司纳入 OpenAI 垂直产品的人才邻接池。

Perplexity 信任 GPT-6 Astra 处理端到端系统

Perplexity trusts GPT-6 Astra with end-to-end systems

Perplexity 使用 Astra 撰写沟通内容、修改软件并监控生产系统，与早期模型相比，需要人工检查的频率大幅降低。

英文原文 EN

Perplexity uses Astra to write communications, change software, and monitor production systems, and checks in much less frequently than with earlier models.

洞察真正重要的信号不是单项 benchmark，而是企业开始把生产系统权限交给模型，并用人工介入频率衡量可信度。这会把竞争门槛推向长程任务可靠性、工具权限治理、可观测性与故障恢复。
招聘行动招聘侧应新增 Agent Reliability、AI SRE、工具调用安全与评测工程人才池，并在候选人访谈中重点核验其生产权限设计、回滚机制、离线评测和线上事故处置经验。

Databricks CEO 谈 AI 发展节奏、网络风险与企业落地

Databricks CEO on AI Pacing, Cyber Risk, and the Enterprise

Databricks 联合创始人兼 CEO Ali Ghodsi 讨论企业 AI 落地的核心瓶颈：模型能力已足够强，但缺少会议历史、决策逻辑与组织经验等上下文。访谈进一步分析组织本体、多模型与 harness 架构、agents 对基础设施和成本的影响，以及递归式自我改进、网络攻击和前沿 AI 是否应放缓等安全问题。

洞察企业 Agent 的护城河正在上移到上下文系统：谁能把分散数据、权限、业务语义和历史决策组织成可用且可治理的状态，谁就更可能让模型产生稳定价值。这意味着数据平台、知识图谱、语义层、权限治理与 Agent Harness 正在汇合成新的基础设施栈。
招聘行动扩大对企业上下文工程人才的搜索，目标包括数据平台、知识图谱、元数据治理、语义层、检索系统、权限控制和 Agent Harness 负责人。候选人评估应减少单纯模型指标权重，增加其把组织数据转化为可靠工作流、控制推理成本并满足安全要求的实战证据。

与 Accenture 合作开展嵌入式评估

Partnering with Accenture on embedded evaluation

Anthropic 与 Accenture 建立非独家合作，由 Accenture 旗下专业 AI 部门 Faculty 牵头开展模型测试、对抗测试、对齐审查与安全防护评估。双方预计未来五年各投入至少 10 亿美元扩充评估能力；嵌入式评估人员可获得接近员工级别的访问权限，但模型安全责任仍由 Anthropic 承担。

洞察前沿模型公司的评估职能正从内部研究小组扩展为可规模化的专业服务与独立验证体系。竞争焦点不再只是基准分数，而是能否持续证明模型在真实行业环境中的可靠性、合规性和抗攻击能力；这会催生模型评估、红队、安全工程与行业专家交叉的新人才市场。
招聘行动建立 AI Evaluation 人才地图，优先覆盖 Anthropic、Faculty、Accenture、METR、Apollo Research 及头部模型实验室的评估负责人、红队研究员和安全工程师；标签中增加嵌入式评估、对抗测试、行业合规和评估平台四类能力，并重点识别兼具客户部署经验与模型技术深度的人选。

认识 ChatGPT Work 中的数据智能体

Meet the Data Agent in ChatGPT Work

借助 ChatGPT Work 中的数据智能体，将业务问题转化为清晰的后续行动。它可用于分析公司数据、构建交互式仪表盘、监控变化，并创建共享上下文以确保答案一致。数据解决方案详情见原文链接。

英文原文 EN

Turn business questions into clear next steps with the Data agent in ChatGPT Work. See how to analyze company data, build interactive dashboards, monitor changes, and create shared context for consistent answers. Explore data solutions: https://openai.com/business/solutions/data/

洞察数据分析 Agent 正从一次性生成 SQL 或图表，演进为连接业务语义、持续监控和团队行动的闭环产品。它压缩了传统 BI、分析工程和业务运营之间的交付链条，也提高了对语义建模、数据可信度与人机审核设计的要求。
招聘行动优先盘点同时具备 Analytics Engineering、语义层、数据产品和 Agent 工作流经验的人才，尤其关注能将业务指标定义、数据权限与自动化行动打通的产品型工程师。招聘画像应加入自助分析采用率、指标一致性和人工审核机制等成果证据。

衡量前沿实验室内部 AI 发展速度的方法

Measurements for understanding the pace of AI development inside frontier labs

Anthropic 提议前沿实验室定期披露 AI 驱动研发、Agent 监督与算力分配指标，并接受独立评估。其内部测量显示，Claude 参与了 26% 的研发工作，约 3 万个 Agent 并发运行；安全工作约占 AI 研发算力的 6%，占 AI 驱动型 AI 研发算力的 12%。

洞察前沿实验室开始把 Agent 规模、自动化等级和安全算力占比变成可度量的组织指标，说明竞争单位正从模型参数转向人机协同研发系统。能够设计 Agent harness、监督机制、评估体系和高并发基础设施的人才，将直接影响实验室的研发吞吐量与风险边界。
招聘行动人才 mapping 应新增 Agent 研发基础设施、自动化评估、监督与升级机制、安全算力治理四类标签；重点追踪 Anthropic 内部平台、评估科学、研究工程和安全工程团队，并识别有大规模异步 Agent 编排经验的技术负责人。

AI 视频的下一前沿是可控性

The Next Frontier of AI Video Is Control

fal 联合创始人 Gorkem Yurtseven 与工程负责人 Batuhan Taskaya 解析 H3 Max：通过对 MiniMax 开放权重视频模型进行 post-training，并联合优化系统与硬件，在保持质量的同时降低生成延迟。访谈还讨论连续视频记忆、实时指令响应，以及镜头、光照、角色动作和 lip sync 的精确控制如何进入专业创作流程。

洞察视频模型的商业分水岭正从能否生成转向能否精确控制、快速迭代并嵌入专业创作流程。模型后训练、推理系统与 GPU 优化的协同，可能比继续堆叠基础模型参数更直接地决定产品体验和单位经济性。
招聘行动在视频生成方向建立模型后训练、扩散模型推理、CUDA 与 GPU 系统、实时渲染和专业创作工具五类人才池。重点识别既能优化延迟与成本，又理解镜头语言、角色一致性和创作者工作流的跨栈人才。

Noam Brown：Agent 集群、alignment 与递归式自我改进

Noam Brown – Agent swarms, alignment, & recursive self-improvement

OpenAI 研究员 Noam Brown 讨论多 agent 系统、用 10,000 个 agents 攻克 Navier–Stokes 问题，以及数学能力跃升对自动化 AI 研究和递归式自我改进的启示。访谈进一步分析 AI 公司的未来协作形态、模型能力差距、思维链可读性退化，以及启动递归式自我改进前应达到的 alignment 标准。

洞察规模化推理正在从增加单次采样转向组织大规模 Agent 协作。真正的瓶颈将落在任务分解、验证、通信、资源调度和安全约束上，因此未来核心团队可能更像分布式系统与研究自动化团队，而非只做模型训练的传统算法组。
招聘行动人才 mapping 应从单模型推理研究员扩展到多 Agent 协作、搜索与规划、形式化验证、分布式调度和 AI for Science 人才。重点追踪有大规模实验编排、自动定理证明、强化学习及复杂系统经验的研究工程师，并在面试中考察其验证失败路径的能力。

★ 本周重点Frontier Radar学术界论文研究技术社区/论文大模型架构推理成本多模态AI Agent

DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash：突破 KV Cache 压缩极限

DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash: Pushing the Limits of KV Cache Compression

长程智能体普及后，模型负载日益偏重输入。尽管长上下文计算成本已显著下降，预填充仍很昂贵，大型 KV Cache 也持续挤压 HBM、SSD 容量及数据传输带宽，计算、存储和带宽共同成为进一步降低部署成本的主要瓶颈。DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 是一款拥有 552B 骨干参数并支持长上下文的多模态混合专家(MoE)模型。

英文原文 EN

The widespread adoption of long-horizon agents has made model workloads increasingly input-heavy. Although prior work has substantially reduced the cost of long-context computation, prefill remains computationally expensive, and large KV caches continue to strain HBM and SSD capacity and data-transfer bandwidth. Together, these compute, storage, and bandwidth demands constitute the primary bottleneck to further lowering deployment costs. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, a multimodal Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model with 552B backbone parameters and support for context

洞察长周期 Agent 的成本瓶颈正在从纯算力扩展为计算、存储与带宽的联合约束。DeepSeek 把预填充激活规模和持久 KV 占用同时压低，说明下一轮推理竞争将由模型架构与部署系统共同决定，懂模型但不懂系统或懂系统但不懂工作负载都不足以形成优势。
招聘行动优先 mapping KV Cache 压缩、稀疏注意力、MoE serving、低精度量化、HBM 调度和存储分层人才，并追踪论文作者及其基础设施合作者；面试评估应加入百万上下文 Agent 负载下的全栈成本设计题。
arXiv cs.CL · 2026-09-17 原文 ↗

GLM 如何自建推理基础设施

How GLM built its own inference infrastructure

GLM 团队复盘自建推理基础设施的工程路径，展现模型公司通过掌握推理栈支撑性能优化与迭代效率的实践。

洞察推理基础设施已经从采购通用 serving 框架转向围绕自有模型特性进行软硬件协同。高社区讨论度说明行业普遍面临同类瓶颈，自建能力会直接改变模型公司的单位经济性和交付边界。
招聘行动人才 mapping 可重点覆盖推理引擎、算子优化、调度编排、GPU 性能工程与容量规划岗位，并将模型团队和基础设施团队之间的协作经验设为高权重筛选条件。

★ 本周重点Frontier RadarOpenAI产品发布创始人/核心成员推理成本推理训练方法产品方向

Sam Altman：前沿强化学习运行前应建立明确安全论证

The world deserves confidence that American companies developing increasingly capable AI will act responsibly, especially as the trajectory of progress has steepened. Every frontier lab must deliver on this, and there is no reason any of us should come to work if we cannot. We welcome a federal framework that sets consistent safety requirements for frontier AI. But we do not believe we need to wait for an anti-trust exemption or legislation to begin the work of providing this confidence. Consistent rules to manage frontier risk so that we can maximize the benefits are a good idea (and we are excited by ideas like independent auditors). Years ago, companies like ours developed things like Responsible Scaling Policies and Preparedness Frameworks. Those were good for that moment, and focused primarily on the deployment of completed models, not what happens during their development process. Today's shift to focusing on safe development and evaluation will need new tools. For example, at OpenAI we now formulate explicit safety cases in advance of frontier reinforcement learning runs we expect to significantly increase capability, in addition to the safety work we have long done in advance of model releases. We hope that other companies will learn from our approaches and propose their own; we think shared standards for misalignment, monitoring, and safety will lead to better outcomes. We look forward to collaborating with our colleagues across the industry to formulate the best version of these. When we talk about “pacing”, we do not mean “stopping”. Progress has been rapid and will continue to be. But it should be slower than it otherwise could be; interventions like safety cases and monitoring have significant costs. Pacing will be well worth this cost; no amount of American competitive pressure should justify recklessness, or let capabilities get ahead of alignment and monitoring. Where we will need the help of our government is for international coordination. But first we should do what we can ourselves.

Sam Altman 主张为前沿 AI 建立一致的安全要求、独立审计与监控机制；OpenAI 已在预计会显著提升能力的前沿 RL 训练前制定明确安全论证。

洞察安全治理正在从模型发布前评估前移到训练运行前决策，意味着风险论证、监控方案和能力阈值将进入研发主流程。安全岗位因而更接近训练平台和决策系统，而非发布后的合规附属职能。
招聘行动人才 mapping 应区分政策型安全与技术型安全，重点寻找具备 frontier evals、红队、监控、形式化风险论证及训练治理经验的人才，并关注独立审计生态的新岗位需求。
Sam Altman · 2026-09-14 原文 ↗
★ 本周重点Frontier RadarxAI观点访谈创始人/核心成员AI编码训练方法算力基建Scaling

Elon Musk：预训练已切换至自研 C/C++ 栈，RL 栈仍待重构

Our training (aka pre-training) software is now the internally developed C/C++ stack, which was written primarily by the Starlink software team. This is awesome and probably best in industry. However, our reinforcement learning stack is still somewhat of a legacy pile of many different third-party software systems. This will get fixed, but isn’t yet.

Elon Musk 表示 xAI 的预训练已采用由 Starlink 软件团队主导开发的 C/C++ 栈，但 RL 栈仍依赖多套遗留第三方系统，后续将进行重构。

洞察前沿实验室正在把训练软件栈视为核心模型资产，自研底层系统可减少框架开销并强化软硬件协同；与此同时，强化学习基础设施成为明显短板，显示后训练系统的复杂度正在追赶预训练。
招聘行动应扩大对 HPC、编译器、CUDA/C++、分布式训练与 RL 平台工程师的扫描，并重点关注航天、云基础设施和高性能计算团队向基础模型公司的跨行业流动。
Elon Musk · 2026-09-14 原文 ↗
★ 本周重点Frontier RadarxAI技术突破创始人/核心成员AI编码训练方法算力基建Scaling

Elon Musk：Grok 4.8 本周完成预训练并启动 RL

@techdevnotes Grok 4.8, which is a 2.5T model trained with our new C++ software stack, will finish training this week and start RL

Elon Musk 表示 Grok 4.8 为 2.5T 模型，使用新的 C++ 软件栈训练，将于本周完成预训练并进入 RL 阶段。

洞察xAI 把核心训练栈进一步下沉到 C++，表明超大规模训练的竞争正在逼近编译器、通信、内存管理和内核级优化。模型规模只是表象，真正稀缺的是能够共同优化算法、系统与硬件利用率的复合型团队。
招聘行动应扩大对训练系统人才的定向 mapping，重点覆盖分布式训练框架、CUDA 与 GPU kernel、通信库、编译器和大规模故障恢复背景。招聘画像需增加端到端吞吐提升、集群有效利用率和万卡级稳定性等可验证指标，而不只看模型论文。

Personal agents are the ultimate manifestation of “build something that agents want”. The form factor of a product like Muse is you want to be able to hand off a task to the agent and ensure that it is fully completed end to end. To do this, the agent must be able to successfully operate with your tools or use its own to complete the task. Use your MCP or CLI, easily navigate your site, be able to transact, and more. The new attention you need to compete for is not from the user itself but instead for the agent. This means that the tools that allow agents to order food, handle ecommerce transactions, book flights, work with the local economy, and interact with our data and information best, are the ones that will get used the most. This will ultimately be the biggest opportunity and shakeup in consumer tech since the App Store itself.

洞察分发入口可能从人类界面迁移到 agent 的工具选择，接口可靠性、可验证执行和机器可读能力将与品牌同样重要。
招聘行动人才 mapping 应重点覆盖 agent 基础设施、MCP、自动交易、工具调用和端到端评测人才，并关注能把传统服务改造成 agent 可操作接口的产品负责人。
X / Aaron Levie · 2026-09-19 ♥ 340 · ↻ 40 · 💬 48 原文 ↗
Follow Builders社区/其他Builder 推文Builder 声音

AI 投资者必须亲手构建才能保持判断力

I spend ~2 hours a day programming, building side projects, tinkering obsessively, trying new repos.. I get basically a

FPV Ventures 合伙人 Nikunj Kothari 每天约花两小时编程、试项目和新仓库。他认为理解 AI 投资机会需要同时跟踪模型能力、长时任务所需基础设施，以及支撑自主公司的 harness 能力，而亲手实践是跟上快速变化的最好方式。

英文原文 EN

I spend ~2 hours a day programming, building side projects, tinkering obsessively, trying new repos.. I get basically a DM a day asking “aren’t you a VC, why are you doing this?” Unlike other eras, things are moving SO fast that if you don’t do this, I simply don’t understand how folks will ever learn what’s coming next. For me, it’s quite simple if you want to invest in the AI era: 1) You have to understand the model capabilities to see where the next set of models will improve - still so much alpha in reading technical papers (model research) 2) You need to see what tools do they need to be successful and complete even longer long horizon tasks (infra) 3) You need to understand the harness capabilities that will make these models help building autonomous companies (app layer) You can obviously learn this by talking to researchers / builders and simply betting on founders - and I do that too. But, I was never honestly good at learning without getting my hands dirty so just leaning in to what I know best. It’s also honestly so fun! So, my push to other folks who want to learn is just get started and find time to tinker daily. Once you do it for 10 days, I’m sure you’ll be equally obsessed!

洞察AI 时代的投资与用人判断越来越依赖一线产品体验，纯二手信息难以识别模型、基础设施和应用层之间的真实瓶颈。
招聘行动评估 AI 投资或战略人才时，询问其最近十天亲手构建和测试过什么，并核验其对模型能力边界的具体理解。

An Empirical Study of Harness Design for Coding Agents

VABench：通过视觉示范、主动感知与度量控制评测具身空间智能

VABench: Measuring Embodied Spatial Intelligence through Visual Demonstrations, Active Perception, and Metric Control

VABench 评估多模态大模型在不完整观察下执行“观察—推理—行动—修正”闭环的能力，涵盖 14 类基础任务、7 类未见几何或布局变体及长程组合任务。实验显示主动相机控制可显著提高成功率，但最佳模型的三次运行宏平均成功率仍仅为 53.93%，严格长程任务无人完成。

Why Diffusion Will Win AI Inference with Inception Co-Founder and CEO Stefano Ermon

核心观点：Inception 联合创始人兼 CEO、Stanford 教授 Stefano Ermon 认为，速度与效率将决定未来几年 AI 推理竞争。其团队从 2019 年开始研究基于去噪的 score-based 生成模型，并把扩散方法从图像推进到文本与代码；2024 年的研究首次在 GPT-2 规模上让扩散式 transformer 达到与自回归模型相当的困惑度。扩散模型可并行生成并具备内置纠错潜力，可能改变文本和代码推理路径。原话是：I was thinking more from a world models perspective。

英文原文 EN

Agents already make up the majority of inference. This will quickly trend toward nearly all inference over the next year or two. The vast majority of tokens used in the world will be agents that are executing unbelievable amounts of tasks for us in the background 24/7. Agents will be deployed to read all code changes to secure our software, process all of our data inside of workflows, handle a significant majority of the research that goes into recruiting and customer prospecting, review every event stream and log from every system in the world, go out and execute tasks for us in our personal lives, and hundreds of other use cases. The rate of new agents coming online that will be consuming insane amounts of tokens is not slowing down. Just in the past week I’ve introduced multiple completely new workflows that would not have been possible technically even a month ago. Incredible time to be doing anything in inference and of course building on top of all of this.

洞察推理需求从交互式请求转向后台持续任务后，成本、调度、缓存、权限和可观测性会成为核心基础设施。

Frontier Radar学术界论文研究技术社区/论文AI Agent推理AI编码训练方法

面向安全机器人操作的障碍感知型编码智能体框架

Coding Agents with an Obstacle-Aware Harness for Safe Robot Manipulation

编码智能体可由语言模型编写机器人控制程序，并在无需机器人专项训练的情况下执行操作任务。研究在每项任务中加入不可触碰的障碍物进行安全评估，发现智能体多数情况下只关注完成目标而忽视安全，导致与障碍物碰撞。

英文原文 EN

Coding agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation: a language model writes the robot controller as a program, and agents built in this way now operate robots without robot-specific training.Whether this paradigm is also safe, however, has not been asked. We evaluate coding agent under a safety constraint, where each task pairs a manipulation goal with an obstacle the robot must not touch. The agent pursues the goal but collides with the obstacle in most cases, treating task completion as its sole objective while neglecting safety. The agent reasons about the obstacle in

Frontier Radar学术界论文研究技术社区/论文推理成本多模态推理AI编码

Workspace Models：通过显著性驱动监督实现轻量级机器人记忆

Workspace Models: Lightweight Robotic Memory via Saliency-Driven Supervision

复杂机器人操作需要长期记忆，但直接使用完整历史容易引入伪相关并降低策略性能。该研究提出在训练阶段利用计算密集型 VLM 查询学习轻量级潜在记忆，从而在部署时高效检索与任务相关的信息。

英文原文 EN

Complex robotic manipulation tasks frequently require a long-term memory of past events and actions. As conditioning on full histories renders policies prone to spurious correlations and degrades performance, many approaches to policy memory involve compressing historical information through expensive VLM queries in-the-loop to process only task-salient information. In this paper, we propose an alternative approach in which computationally intensive VLM queries are made during train-time to learn a lightweight latent memory that can be efficiently queried at deployment time. Our representation

Frontier Radar学术界论文研究技术社区/论文大模型架构推理训练方法Scaling

FAMOS：基于稀疏观测的前馈式三维关节结构建模

FAMOS: Feed-Forward 3D Articulation Modeling from Sparse Observations

稀疏单目视图只能呈现局部几何与运动信息，使关节物体建模颇具挑战。FAMOS 从无序、稀疏的局部点云集合预测可动部件分割与关节参数，可联合推理多次观测，并自然支持包括单视图在内的可变输入数量。

英文原文 EN

Modeling articulated objects from sparse monocular views is challenging because each observation reveals only partial geometry and motion evidence. Most feed-forward methods infer articulation from a single observation and therefore rely heavily on learned category-level shape priors. We present FAMOS, a feed-forward model that predicts movable-part segmentation and joint parameters from a sparse, unordered set of partial point clouds. 研究探讨如何从稳态观测与干预数据中恢复多元 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（OU）过程参数。在大规模基因扰动等应用中，通常只有静态「快照」测量，依赖时间序列轨迹的标准随机微分方程估计方法并不适用。研究证明，对漂移图的每个强连通分量（SCC）实施一次干预，通常足以恢复全部 OU 过程参数。

JustMem：面向长期对话的恰到好处记忆访问

JustMem: Just-Enough Memory Access for Long-Term Conversations

高效的长期对话记忆需要检索充分证据，同时避免无差别扩张语言模型上下文。相关证据可能分散在多个会话中，而压缩又可能丢失回答所需细节，因此不同查询需要不同的记忆访问方式。JustMem 从两个维度刻画这一需求：发现广度控制证据搜索范围，读取保真度控制证据的读取精细程度。

英文原文 EN

Efficient long-term conversational memory requires retrieving sufficient evidence without indiscriminately expanding the context presented to the language model. CARE-VI：用于离策略 Actor-Critic 学习价值改进的保守自适应可靠性估计

CARE-VI: Conservative Adaptive Reliability Estimation for Value Improvement in Off-Policy Actor-Critic Learning

可靠的时序差分目标是离策略 Actor-Critic 学习的核心。直接价值改进通过备选动作优化下一状态目标，但其可靠性取决于候选动作的排序、审查和加权。噪声排序可能导致过早选定候选，复用选择分数会使目标估值产生偏差，固定增强权重也可能放大弱证据。为此，研究提出保守自适应排序与筛选（CARS），在预设预算内保留有序候选前缀。

英文原文 EN

Reliable temporal-difference targets are central to off-policy actor-critic learning. Noisy rankings may force premature candidate commitment, reusing selection scores may bias target valuation, and fixed enhancement weights may amplify weak evidence. 当前正确、未来不足：审计上下文压缩的更新充分性

Correct Now, Insufficient Later: Auditing Update Sufficiency in Context Compression

记忆可能正确回答当前查询，却丢失未来更新所需的关键区别。研究通过成对历史审计这一问题：两段历史当前答案相同，接受相同的后续更新后，却应产生不同答案。试验覆盖六种合成机制下的 24 对历史、12 种记忆条件、两次重复和两个模型后端。确定性前沿选择器在 DeepSeek 上严格揭示准确率为 96/96，在 GLM 上为 82/96；结构化写入器分别取得 62 次成功和 56/96 的结果。

英文原文 EN

A memory can answer a current query correctly while discarding distinctions required by a later update. A pilot evaluates 24 history pairs across six synthetic mechanisms, 12 memory conditions, two repeats, and two model backends. FPV Ventures 合伙人 Nikunj Kothari 构建了健康儿童零食目录 nosugarforkids，并让 Claude agent 每日检查商品、清理失效条目、寻找内容选题、分析 DataForSEO 与 Google Search Console、撰写和编辑内容并联系媒体获取反向链接。在没有反向链接和社交账号的情况下，网站已自然增长到每天约 6000 次曝光和 60 次点击。

英文原文 EN

I have been obsessed with self driving companies for more than a year now.. tried a LOT of products, none of them met the bar and decided to just build one myself with "claude" in the loop. Introducing my first experiment: nosugarforkids (dot com) 🙅‍♂️ > catalog of only healthy snacks for kids (surprisingly this didn't exist) > you can chat to get recommendations for school, lunches, snack boxes > built a "tier" list based on the nutrients and what's actually good On the autonomous side, I have a Claude agent that wakes up once a day to do the following things: > checks catalog for new additions > prunes any products that may be dead > finds new content ideas to help increase reach > checks dataforSEO and Google Search Console for performance > writes and edits new pieces that might be useful to help > keeps quality high, and tries to not introduce slop > (new) reaches out to publications to get backlinks Even with ZERO backlinks today and no social presence whatsoever, site has organically grown to ~6k impressions a day and 60 clicks. Lots of rough edges still, but parents give it a try and tell me if it's helpful!

洞察小型网站已经能由 agent 持续完成内容、SEO 和目录维护，验证了低人力自治运营的可行性，但质量控制仍是明确难点。
招聘行动寻找有 agent 增长、内容运营自动化和质量治理经验的复合人才，并把实际流量与维护指标纳入项目核验。

AI for Science with GPT-6 Astra: Thermal Design and Electrothermal Analysis of 2D CFET

2D CFET 反相器的热优化需要权衡结构方案及其电气成本。我们在给定的电热模型中使用 AI 智能体工作流研究这些任务。在 12 nm 尺度下，Astra 选择了重新分布的源极互连结构，协调智能体则提出了一条面向衬底的散热路径。组合设计在金属体积固定、功率为 20 μW 时将峰值温升降低 1.67 K。后续金属电阻敏感性分析显示，反相器可降温约 0.6 K，同时 nFET 导通电流损失 2%。有效接触长度缩放进一步……

We examine these research tasks using an AI agent workflow within a supplied electrothermal model. The combined design reduces peak temperature rise by 1.67 K at fixed metal volume and 20 μW. 符号分离：以知识图谱锚定深度智能体，实现可信运营数据分析

Symbolic Separation: Grounding Deep Agents in Knowledge Graphs for Trustworthy Operational Data Analytics

生成式 AI 有望让用户通过自然语言访问数据中心和工业 4.0 设施中的海量数值遥测数据，但文本到查询和工具调用智能体仍不可靠：即使是前沿模型，对真实数据库问题的回答率也仅略高于一半，对多步骤运营问题的回答率更低。这是因为 LLM 必须推断异构数据源之间的关系，并会在关系而非仅字段上产生幻觉。我们提出符号分离：深度智能体可以自由推理，但只能通过受本体约束、具有确定性预……的虚拟知识图谱对数据执行操作。

英文原文 EN

Generative AI promises natural language access to the massive numerical telemetry of data centers and Industry 4.0 installations, yet text-to-query and tool-using agents stay unreliable: even frontier models answer little more than half of real-world database questions, and far fewer of the multi-step, operational ones, because the LLM must compose how heterogeneous sources relate and hallucinates the relations, not just the fields. Scaling-Score Conformal Prediction for Multi-Target Regression

多目标回归要求模型同时预测多个相关输出。保形预测可提供无分布、有限样本的边际覆盖保证，但要以模型无关且样本高效的方式将其扩展到联合多维区域仍具挑战：最大值聚合忽略尺度差异；基于 copula 的方法仅渐近有效；矩形方法通常会拆分校准集；基于分位数或密度的方法则要求在普通点预测器之外训练专用模型。我们提出缩放分数保形……

英文原文 EN

Multi-target regression requires a model to simultaneously predict several related outputs. Conformal prediction provides distribution-free, finite-sample marginal coverage guarantees, but extending these to joint multi-dimensional regions in a model-agnostic, sample-efficient manner remains challenging: max-aggregation ignores scale differences, copula-based methods are only asymptotically valid, rectangular methods typically split the calibration set, and quantile or density-based methods require training a specialised model beyond a plain point predictor. 面向长期对话的交互式记忆学习

Interactive Memory Learning for Long-Term Conversations

大语言模型的最新进展显著增强了智能体建模长期对话的能力。尽管取得了这些成果，现有方法通常仍采用静态启发式范式，被动归档信息，缺乏自适应记忆价值评估。因此，这些方法无法自我演进，也无法让记忆管理适应不断变化的用户需求。为此，我们提出 ICML（InteraCtive Memory Learning），这是一种多智能体框架，可将记忆机制从被动档案转变为可学习、可交互的记忆策略。

英文原文 EN

Recent advancements in large language models have significantly enhanced the capabilities of agents in modeling long-term conversations. Despite these successes, existing approaches typically adopt a static heuristic paradigm, where information is passively archived without adaptive memory valuation. Consequently, these methods fail to self-evolve or align their memory management with evolving user needs. To address this, we propose ICML (InteraCtive Memory Learning), a multi-agent framework that transforms the memory mechanism from a passive archive into a learnable, interactive memory policy

本文提出低秩分位数曲面（LRQS），一种双变量因果模型：在因果方向上，条件分位数曲面经过未知单调变换后可进行低秩函数分解。LRQS 涵盖位置—尺度噪声模型和后非线性异方差噪声模型，并允许使用多个分位数基来表示超越位置—尺度效应的变化。本文证明了 LRQS 的一般可识别性：变换后的分位数曲面在因果方向上为低秩，而在相应约束下，只有特定情形才能在反向上表示。

英文原文 EN

We propose Low-Rank Quantile Surfaces (LRQS), a bivariate causal model in which, in the causal direction, an unknown monotone transformation of the conditional quantile surface admits a low-rank functional decomposition. LRQS subsumes location-scale noise models and post-nonlinear heteroscedastic noise models, while allowing multiple quantile bases to represent changes beyond location-scale effects. We prove generic identifiability of LRQS: the transformed quantile surface is low rank in the causal direction, whereas reverse representability under the corresponding constraints occurs only for

When Confidence Signals Disagree: Local and Global Confidence in Autoregressive Language Models

现代预测系统会输出多种通常被解释为置信度的量，但这些量可能概括预测过程的不同方面。当置信度用于评估可靠性或指导下游监督与控制时，这一区别至关重要。本文通过比较局部置信度与全局置信度，研究自回归语言模型中的不同置信度读数在经验上能否互换：前者定义为贪心选中答案 token 的概率，后者定义为重复采样中众数答案出现的频率。

英文原文 EN

Modern predictive systems expose multiple quantities that are commonly interpreted as measures of confidence. However, these quantities can summarize different aspects of the predictive process. This distinction matters when confidence is used to evaluate reliability or inform downstream oversight and control. Show HN：Sunk Cost——本地 LLM 设备多久能回本？

Show HN: Sunk Cost – How long until a local LLM rig pays for itself?

Sunk Cost 根据设备、模型和每日词元用量，计算购买硬件相较于按词元租用同一模型需要多久才能回本。购买自有硬件除节省 LLM 使用成本外还有其他理由，但该工具仅从直接成本节省角度进行评估。

英文原文 EN

I kept hearing "just buy a Mac and run models locally, it pays for itself" and wanted to check. Sunk Cost takes a machine, a model and how many tokens you use a day, and works out how long the hardware takes to pay back against renting the same model by the token. Obviously there are other reasons to buy your own hardware aside from just saving money on llms but this is just looking at it from a raw cost saving perspective. If you have any ideas on how I can make this more helpful lmk!

Follow Builders社区/其他Builder 推文Builder 声音

agent 工作负载可能比预期扩大百倍

We probably need to all update our sense of what’s coming in terms of agentic workloads with the combination of agent sw

Box CEO Aaron Levie 认为，agent swarm、更强计算机使用、新 API 与 MCP、个人和企业 agent 会让后台任务量远超最初想象。他预测 agent 将全天候精准招聘、捕捉客户销售信号、分析全部对话、审查每行代码并进行大规模测试，而行业可能只走完形态探索的 1%。

英文原文 EN

We probably need to all update our sense of what’s coming in terms of agentic workloads with the combination of agent swarms, better computer use, the next wave of APIs and MCPs coming online, and new form factors like Muse or Instinct, vertical enterprise agents, background workflow agents, and other products that are emerging right now. We’re going to throw agents at vastly more tasks in our professional and personal lives than we had initially imagined. The kind of information that agents will go out and find for us and do work for us in the background is going to be 100X more volume than what we can imagine previously prompting in a single session. You’re going to have agents go out and surgically recruit talent for you 24/7, look for every signal in your customer’s business for when to pitch them, process every single transcript and conversation for product insights, review every line of code written for security issues and bugs, brute force test your systems for issues, and 100s of other tasks. We’re probably 1% of the way into the shape of what all these agents look like, where they get deployed from, how they get managed, how we budget for them, and so on. But it’s inevitably going to happen at a scale that wouldn’t have been fathomable before.

洞察agent 规模化不仅是模型问题，还要求预算、调度、权限、管理和结果验证形成完整控制面。
招聘行动招聘团队可优先试点全天候人才搜寻 agent，同时重点 mapping agent orchestration、observability 与治理平台人才。

Sam Altman：AI 失控与权力过度集中是两类核心风险

There are two ways AI progress could go very badly and that we must avoid. First, we could lose control of the future to AI. This is unacceptable; we are unapologetically on Team Humanity, and AI must always serve people. To ensure that, we need ways to ensure that alignment and safety techniques stay ahead of progress in model capabilities. Second, we could end up in a world with too much concentration of power. If an extraordinarily powerful AI is used by one person or company to impress their worldview onto everyone else, the results could be extremely dystopian. Avoiding these two threats requires walking a narrow middle path; for example, one country could gain too much power. Another example is one lab ending up with too much power.

Sam Altman 认为 AI 必须始终服务人类，对齐与安全技术需要领先于模型能力，同时应避免强大 AI 的控制权过度集中于单一国家、个人或实验室。

GSLAD: Prototype-Regularized Graph Structure Learning for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection

However, industrial faults may first alter inter-variable structural patterns while individual trajectories remain close to normal, resulting in weak anomaly signals. In this paper, we propose GSLAD, a prototype-regularized graph structure learning framework that uses structural deviations for anomaly scoring. GSLAD adopts a two-phase training strategy. 分词器在 ECG Transformer 模型中的作用

On the role of the tokenizer in ECG transformer models

分词方式决定了 ECG Transformer 接收的生理信息及注意力作用的序列。研究在九标签 CPSC2018 分类任务上，对 Transformer、Informer、Reformer 和 FEDformer 的八种分词策略进行比较，并控制输入投影与主干模型容量以隔离词元构造的影响。Median-beat 与 HeartLang 在四种主干上的平均宏观 AUC 分别为 0.893 和 0.889，高于逐点分词与分块分词的 0.822 和 0.824。

英文原文 EN

Tokenization determines both the physiological content presented to an ECG Transformer and the sequence over which attention operates. We compare eight tokenization strategies across Transformer, Informer, Reformer, and FEDformer on the nine-label CPSC2018 classification task. The input projection and principal backbone capacity are controlled to isolate the effect of token construction. Median-beat and HeartLang tokenization achieve mean macro-AUCs of 0.893 and 0.889 across the four backbones, compared with 0.822 and 0.824 for point-wise and patch-wise tokenization. Single-condition neural solvers encode transferable response spaces for parametric differential equations

参数化偏微分方程(PDE)的算子学习通常需在预设域上构建全局模型，依赖跨条件数据或成本高昂的物理约束训练。研究表明，在单一条件下训练的神经解模型，其输出雅可比矩阵可定义可复用的跨条件解变化响应空间。为此提出线性化子空间迁移(LST)，通过在响应空间坐标上最小化目标 PDE 系统残差来恢复目标解。

英文原文 EN

Operator learning for parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) typically builds global models over prescribed domains, requiring cross-condition data or costly physics-constrained training. Here we show that the output Jacobian of a neural solution model trained at one condition defines a reusable response space for cross-condition solution variations. We introduce Linearized Subspace Transfer (LST) to exploit this space and recover target solutions by minimizing the target PDE-system residual over response-space coordinates. 安全强化学习的评估指标

Evaluation Metrics for Safe Reinforcement Learning

安全强化学习(RL)通常被形式化为约束马尔可夫决策过程(CMDP)：智能体在最大化期望奖励的同时，将期望累计成本控制在给定安全界限内。现有基准主要依据这一期望保证报告算法平均是否安全，但该做法无法可靠刻画真实安全性，例如违反安全界限的频率与严重程度，以及结果能否在不同任务和安全界限下保持一致。

英文原文 EN

Safe reinforcement learning (RL) is commonly formalized as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP), in which an agent maximizes expected reward while keeping its expected cumulative cost below a specified safety bound. Existing safe RL benchmarks predominantly report whether an algorithm is safe on average, following this expectation-based guarantee. 要将强化学习算法应用于现实场景，必须确保其在不利运行条件下仍具安全性。研究聚焦对抗性特征缺失：攻击者遮蔽智能体观测中的特征，以尽可能降低其性能。论文正式定义强化学习中的对抗性缺失，将其与 L∞ 范数有界对抗扰动及缺失数据学习进行比较，并提出一种对抗训练算法。

大语言模型通过从预测分布中逐词元采样来生成文本，温度参数决定采样偏离最高概率词元的程度。top-p、min-p 等截断采样器会在采样前剔除最低概率词元，以维持高温度下的连贯性。其已报告的准确率增益多来自 1.5 至 3 的温度，而部署系统的默认值集中在 0.6 至 1.0。研究在 GSM8K 上测试了 13 个开放权重模型，以衡量截断采样在默认温度下对不同模型准确率的影响。

英文原文 EN

Large language models generate text by sampling each token from a predicted distribution, and a temperature parameter sets how far the draw strays from the most probable tokens. Truncation samplers such as top-p and min-p discard the least probable tokens before the draw, so that sampling at high temperature stays coherent. Their reported accuracy gains come from temperatures of 1.5 to 3, while the defaults of deployed systems cluster between 0.6 and 1.0. 生成式热力学计算机通过 Langevin 动力学将热噪声转化为结构化数据。研究在每个积分步骤采用局部更新，并利用反向路径 Onsager-Machlup 目标得到由局部残差—状态相关性对称求和构成的耦合梯度，随后立即应用该梯度，而非在完整轨迹上累积。基于 MNIST 原型的数字仿真显示，在固定加噪路径上，在线训练与轨迹批训练的验证损失相近，而在线训练模型的平均放热更少。

英文原文 EN

Generative thermodynamic computers turn thermal noise into structured data through Langevin dynamics. We train these systems with a local update at each integration step. The reverse-path Onsager-Machlup objective yields a coupling gradient that is a symmetric sum of local residual-state correlations. We apply this gradient immediately rather than accumulating it over a full trajectory. In digital simulations using MNIST prototypes, online and trajectory-batch training reach similar validation losses on fixed noising paths. agent 百倍访问量重塑企业数据安全

Protecting enterprise data in a world where agents are using our systems 100X more than people ever did is going to be o

Box CEO Aaron Levie 认为，当 agent 对企业系统的使用频率达到人类的百倍，数据保护将成为本世纪最复杂的安全与治理挑战之一。权限过宽会失控，完全封锁又无法获得生产力；Box Shield 已按文档分类控制 agent 可访问内容，并探索异常访问检测、告警与阻断。

英文原文 EN

Protecting enterprise data in a world where agents are using our systems 100X more than people ever did is going to be one of the more complex security and governance challenges of the 21st century. Importantly, security and productivity gains are inexorably linked in the world of AI. If you give an agent too much unfettered information access, it will be difficult to truly control and protect your data; and conversely, if you lock everything down completely, you won’t get any real productivity gains from AI. We need all new ways to protect our systems, environments, and structured and unstructured dada in the enterprise in an intelligent way by modernizing our approach to security and governance. At Box, as one example, we’re building new intelligent ways to protect enterprise data and agentic use of that information. A recent update in Box Shield is to provide granular controls on what content agents can and can’t work with based on document classification level. We’re also working on other features that can automatically detect and alert (or block) when data is being accessed or used in unusual or anomalous ways by agents. And this is just the start. There’s a ton more innovation coming across the entire industry - from the labs like OpenAI or Anthropic; security platforms like Palo Alto Networks, Cisco, CrowdStrike, Okta; or startups like Enclave, Method, Alterion, Runlayer, and many many others - to rethink how we protect information in the world of AI agents. Exciting and wild times ahead.

招聘行动优先 mapping 数据安全、IAM、DLP、行为检测和 AI 治理人才，并关注 Box、Palo Alto Networks、Cisco、CrowdStrike、Okta 及相关创业公司。

Piston 用封闭支付网络减少车队燃油欺诈

I have been obsessed with closed payment loops for a LONG time, and have been looking to find a company trying to solve

FPV Ventures 合伙人 Nikunj Kothari 宣布领投 Piston A 轮。Piston 绕开传统卡网络，由平台向加油站付款再向车队开票，并把每笔交易绑定到司机、时间、地点和燃油类型；作者称其支付额增长 8 倍、商户网络增长 40 倍、留存率高于 98%，已覆盖 48 个州约 2000 座加油站。

英文原文 EN

I have been obsessed with closed payment loops for a LONG time, and have been looking to find a company trying to solve a real problem.. excited to finally back Piston (piston dot com) ⛽️ USA alone spends hundreds of billions in fuel. Most of that spend comes through cards (credit, fuel, or sometime house cards). However, a lot of these cards are not even tied to a particular driver. They often get stolen, skimmed, or misused. The fleet owner then has to eat the fraud and then spend a long time reconciling what exactly happened. The gas station has to pay interchange fees to accept the card, but has no relationship with the fleets on the other side. And a lot of companies have come and gone trying to optimize this infrastructure rather than thinking about it from the very beginning. You really can't fix this issue by JUST building a better fuel card. The card is the problem, and so Piston decided to get rid of it. It moves the payment over its own rails without networks or any intermediaries. Piston pays the station and then invoices the fleet. Every transaction is tied to a specific driver with the time, location, and fuel type attached, and soon it'll be integrated with the vehicle itself. The founders faced this problem themselves. @vik_sekhon runs his own fleet of trucks, and his fleet lost real money to fuel fraud. @Shivam2291 used to recruit thousands of drivers before the two of them got together to help build Piston. The best signal for how effective Piston is for fleets is the moment they switch over to Piston, they see their fuel spend automatically go down. I have seen so many charts and customer quotes that show this directly. Customer love shows in the metrics. Payment volume has grown 8x and merchant network has grown 40x while retention has stayed above 98%. Piston is now live across 2,000 stations and across 48 states. Fleets pay nothing for it today and they get fraud prevention, which is what a card-based system cannot offer. Gas stations get direct access to fleets to help build loyalty and offer adjacent products. To increase their reach, Piston is now directly integrated with POS of the majority of gas stations in America. This allows them to turn on hundreds of gas stations at once, secure supply and hence fulfill their already burgeoning demand. Thrilled to be leading their Series A and joining the board, along with existing investors @sparkcapital & @pearvc. Special thanks to @arpans who introduced me to this team last year!

洞察垂直金融科技通过重构支付轨道而非优化卡产品来解决欺诈与对账，创始人的行业亲历是重要产品洞察来源。
招聘行动人才 mapping 可关注垂直支付、风控、POS 集成与车队软件人才，并核验候选人是否具备真实行业数据闭环经验。

DenMark: Robust Semantic Watermarking for Diffusion Language Models

语义文本水印将信号编码在语义而非表层词元选择中，因此能抵抗释义改写等保持语义的编辑。现有方法主要面向自回归语言模型(ARLM)，可在继续生成前完成候选单元的生成与评分，但这一范式难以直接用于扩散语言模型(DLM)，因为语义单元在中间去噪阶段尚不完整，词元更新顺序也更灵活。研究据此提出语义水印框架 DenMark。

英文原文 EN

Semantic text watermarks encode signals in meaning rather than surface token choices, offering robustness to paraphrasing and other semantic-preserving edits. Existing semantic watermarking methods are primarily designed for autoregressive language models (ARLMs), where completed candidate units can be generated and scored before generation proceeds. This paradigm does not naturally extend to diffusion language models (DLMs), where semantic units remain incomplete during intermediate denoising steps and tokens may be updated in flexible orders. 递归式 AI 能否重新加速科学发现

When AI Improves Itself | Richard Socher (Recursive)

核心观点：Richard Socher 认为，可模拟的问题最终都可能被 AI 解决，而 AI 改进 AI 的递归循环会成为重要突破口。Recursive 创始人 Richard Socher 指出，科学知识已从可掌握的体系变成由约 3.4 万种期刊构成的知识迷宫，学科碎片化与学术激励对高风险新想法的不友好共同拖慢了突破。他回顾早期神经网络 NLP 论文屡遭拒稿的经历，强调跨学科连接与突破既有范式的重要性。原话是：Anything you can simulate, AI will solve。

洞察递归自我改进的近期价值不一定是抽象的智能爆炸，而可能首先体现在文献整合、实验模拟和研究工具链自动优化上。
招聘行动招聘与 mapping 可重点寻找同时具备基础模型、科学计算和跨学科研究经历的人才，并用其是否能设计可验证的自改进闭环来区分概念型与工程型候选人。

谁来评测 AI 模型？Ben Horowitz 与 Rayan Krishnan

Who Grades the AI Models? | Ben Horowitz & Rayan Krishnan

Vals 创始人兼 CEO Rayan Krishnan 与 Ben Horowitz 等人讨论公开基准趋于饱和后，为何需要独立且持续更新的模型评测。访谈涵盖发布前快速测试、跨数小时乃至数周的 agentic 系统评测、成本与延迟指标、企业 AI 投资回报，以及评测在模型路由、递归式自我改进和 AI 治理中的作用。

专业写作者如何把 AI 变成思考工具

How a Professional Writer Writes With AI

核心观点：Every 的 Katie Parrott 把 AI 写作视为澄清思想的过程，而不是代替作者给出轻松答案。她从用 ChatGPT 生成重复性内容和充当职业教练开始，逐渐形成把 prompt 当作战略写作的方法，并参与开发 Compound Writing 插件。她强调，AI 可以帮助外化思考、提出问题和保持问责，但洞见仍需要作者亲自挖掘。原话是：The real work is still mine。

洞察高质量 AI 写作流程的核心是把模型放在研究、提问和迭代环节，而不是一次性生成最终文本。
招聘行动评估 AI 内容与知识工作人才时，要求候选人展示从素材、prompt、批判性修订到成稿的完整过程，而非只看最终文案。
